# Game Icons (`gi`) — 4180 icons

- **Prefix:** `Gi` · **Version:** 82d9488 · **License:** CC-BY-3.0 · **Style:** fill · **viewBox:** `0 0 512 512`
- **Source:** [game-icons/icons](https://github.com/game-icons/icons)
- 4180 detailed game/fantasy/RPG icons from game-icons.net (e.g. `GiSwordWound`, `GiDragonHead`, `GiHealthPotion`, `GiShield`). Single-color `currentColor` fill on a 512×512 canvas; theme via `color`.

Import default-only per icon: `import GiExample from "@mal-icons/react/gi/GiExample"`, or named from the set barrel: `import { GiExample } from "@mal-icons/react/gi"`.
